ParlaMint-HR_2014-03-05-0.u73315	Thank you, well-respected Vice-President of the Croatian Parliament, respected MPs and MPs. Protection of persons with disabilities in the Republic of Croatia is ensured in different ways and in different ways, depending on the system in which that protection is achieved. Therefore, the idea of a single body of art for all persons with disabilities has been public for several years. The fragmentation of the system of art, different criteria and excessive administration are facts that make it difficult to access the rights of children with developmental difficulties and people with disabilities. However, there has always been a near-achieving, but never before has there been a clear political will or the strength to revive the valuable project. With this law, the Ministry of Social Policy and Youth shows a strong will to arrange this complicated and for citizens of systematic art. Most of all, hard to access. We listened to the wishes and needs of civil society organisations that collect people with disabilities and who eagerly await this law. We also had full cooperation from several ministries, especially I would point out the Ministry of Labour and Pension System because this is a complex job and the rights of persons with disabilities are not exclusively in the management of social care systems. The fact is that individual rights which allow the provision of independence of persons with disabilities and their social and labour integration, the compensation of adverse situations and the removal of difficulties in participating in the creation and implementation of social policy are established by the basis of a number of regulations governing certain areas of social life. The idea of forming a single body of art is primarily based on the need to determine the physical, intellectual, sensory and mental impairment of health, disability, functional ability, working and remaining working ability of a person by one institution or institution in a two-stage procedure through uniformly established methodologies and on a unique pattern of findings and opinions which avoid the dissonance of previous skills through the witches of each of the individual systems. In accordance with the proposed law, the single body of art would act as a founding unit of the Institute for Arts, Professional Rehabilitation and Employment of Persons with Disabilities that would function at the level of the Republic of Croatia with exposures at the regional, regional or local level. Namely, the Law on Professional Rehabilitation and Employment of Persons with Disabilities provides for the establishment of the Institute of Skills, Professional Rehabilitation and Employment of Persons with Disabilities and the following tasks: skills in the first and second stages of procedures for the exercise of rights in the field of social care, pension insurance, maternity and parental support, education, professional rehabilitation and employment of persons with disabilities, protection of war and civilian war victims and other areas in which certain rights are exercised on the basis of skills, i.e. on the basis of the findings and opinions of the body of art as well as other works related to the skills of a specific regulation. The proposed law responds to the need to establish a system of skills with professionals who are employed in the system for full-time work. In addition to their basic education, the experts will have a unique education on the principles and methodology of art as opposed to the current state according to which, except at the Croatian Pension Insurance Institute, training methodologies are left to members of committees who are professional in their fields but are not educated in the art process. According to the current state, more than 900 part-time experts are used in the social care system alone, which largely raises the quality and uniformity of findings and opinions. It is also noted that in the new system, the applicant at the heart of all the associated institutions is eliminating the person's walk from the institution to the institution in search of his rights. The rights in different systems are also harmonised and informationally linked and co-ordinated by the work of institutions, costs are rationalised and the efficiency of the system is increased in such a way that the associated institutions allow the exercise of rights in each of the systems based on a single finding and the opinion of one institution of the unique body of art. The proposed law is the result of the need to develop uniform criteria for the identification of disability. The process of production began several years ago and, in the opinion of an interdisciplinary team composed of representatives of the Ministry in which the bodies of art act, experts of persons with disabilities, doctors and experts of different areas of disability should take into account the type of damage that led to the disability and the assessment of the functionality of the person and the determination of the level of necessary support. This follows the World Health Organization's recommendations on the need to create a social medical model of disability, which is also an attempt to valorise the definition of disability under the Convention on the Rights of Persons with Disabilities, which is a Croatian signatories. The methodology Regulation to be adopted under this law will contain a single list of damages. Those physical, intellectual, sensory and mental expressed in percentages, as well as the list of functional abilities with parameters adapted to systems in which certain rights for the achievement of which are used are found and the opinion of the unique body of art. The regulation will also determine who is a person with disabilities so that the opinions of the unique body of art will also answer that question. The analysis of the existing state of play with regard to the spending of financial resources has shown that in the relevant systems on the skills of part-time and employee warlocks, nearly 43.5 million kunas per year are spent on about 140 thousand art subjects while an estimated 38 million kuna per year would be spent on the establishment of a single body of artificial arts costs. It is not a significant reduction, but the basic objective of this law is not to rationalise funds but rather to make the system easier and more accessible to citizens who need skills. By adopting this law, all persons with disabilities will in one place carry out an art-making process according to a unique methodology and rights will comply with the rules in the pension insurance system, social care, education, professional rehabilitation and employment of persons with disabilities, the protection of war and civilian victims of war and other areas in which they exercise certain rights based on the findings and opinions of the body of art. In fact, the idea is that experts who are now in certain systems are employed in the work of art are taken over and worked in the body that is designed for the work of art for all systems, i.e. to be employees of the Institute. This body would take over existing experts who would have contracted work with a new institution and it would also be possible to hire additional experts and funds that have been paid so far by external associates, for example many of them, in the social care system. The findings of this body's opinion would be valid in all systems and would allow citizens to have a simple and equal access to rights through a uniform methodology of art, but also to reduce bureaucratic obstacles that we all aim at, so I call on you to support this bill. Thank you very much.

ParlaMint-HR_2014-03-05-0.u73351	Dear Vice-Presidents of the Parliament, respected deputy ministers, colleagues and fellow MPs. When we talk about the proposal of the Law on the Single Body of Art, we need to define ourselves a little bit and look back at what the assessment of the situation is now. First of all, protection of persons with disabilities in the Republic of Croatia is ensured differently depending on the system in which that protection is achieved. Some rights are exercised through a set of rules and regulations. And then it is really necessary to even the rights of the same name and to change existing practice that in different systems, health damage is determined in different ways. In the Republic of Croatia, there are currently 6 bodies of art and that experts in order to exercise rights in the field of social care, pension insurance, education, professional rehabilitation, employment of persons with disabilities, the right to basic maternity and parental support and the protection of war and civilian victims of war, and when determining disability, use different legal regulations in their work. The establishment of a single body of art is based on the UN Convention on the Rights of Persons with Disabilities and the Faculty Protocol with the Convention on the Rights of Persons with Disabilities and on the National Strategy for Equalisation of Opportunities for Persons with Disabilities from 2007 to 2015, which establishes the obligation for competent authorities to develop a basis for improving the rights from the basis of disability and to propose the adoption of appropriate regulations. It also provides for a measure to identify a single body of art. The job of artmaking has so far been done within 6 ministries, more than 1,000 witches were hired to contract the work and more than 40 million kunas were spent annually. The basic issues that we want to regulate under the proposed law, first of all, we want the proposed law to regulate the single body of art as the establishment unit of the Institute for Professional Rehabilitation of Arts and Employment of Persons with Disabilities and to have the activity and jobs of the body of art. Local jurisdiction, the definition of skills and the process of exercise of rights, the experts who conduct the art processes are established and we want to regulate other issues governing the work of the single body of art. The unique body of art has been established by the Office for the Arts, Professional Rehabilitation and Employment of Persons with Disabilities. The activity and competence of the Institute is defined by a special regulation establishing professional rehabilitation and employment of persons with disabilities and covering the following activities: skills in the first and second stages of procedures to determine the rights in the field of social care, pension insurance, education, professional rehabilitation and employment of persons with disabilities and maternity and parental support, protection of war and civilian victims of war and other areas which exercise certain rights based on the findings and opinions of the body of art. It also includes the activities of making proposals and expert opinions related to the implementation of skills and monitoring, organisation and improvement of skills activities with implementation and also education from the same areas of skills. And, of course, other jobs related to just art. The training in the first-degree procedure shall be carried out in the regional establishment units of the institution in the place responsible for the residence of a qualified person and in the second-degree procedure in the Central Office. The unique body of art will be located in the Rehabilitation Fund under the supervision of the Ministry of Labour and Pension System. The bodies of the art provide an opinion and opinion on the prescribed form, keeping records and documentation relating to... by training, working with the authorities of the State Administration for the needs of the Register of Persons with Disabilities and by providing a review of the findings and opinions of the Croatian Institute of Public Health and, once a year, submits a report on its work. They shall also be implemented at the request of the competent authority which decides on the rights of those areas. The skills are performed by doctors of medicine, doctors of dental medicine, social workers, psychologists, experts, educational rehabilitation profile and other professionals as needed.
